# Verbatim `validate_ledger.py --no-baseline` error set immediately BEFORE
# the DEC-20260802-001 relocation.
#
# Snapshot commit: 2225c24d899729eadb12ae60e54682cd98068fa5
#   fix: repair the two SGCP V18 test failures
# This is the parent of 27e38a09, the relocation commit.
#
# WHY THIS FILE EXISTS
#
# DEC-20260802-002 exclusion 3 bars any error line naming a record created by
# the relocation from entering the grandfathering baseline, so the relocation
# cannot smuggle its own new errors in. But debt that merely CHANGED PATH in
# that relocation is not new, and hand-itemizing which lines those are is what
# failed: CORR-20260802-014's carried-forward list was built by predicting
# which targets would register, and it checked that
# experiments/EXP-SIG-008/specification.yaml exists without checking that it
# parses -- so DEC-20260727-012's dangling EXP-SIG-008 target was omitted.
#
# DEC-20260802-004 therefore restates exclusion 3 as a mechanical test: a line
# naming a relocated record is admissible if and only if the identical message
# appears in this file under that record's PRE-relocation path. Set membership,
# not judgement about intent.
#
# This file is evidence, not configuration. It is never edited or regenerated:
# regenerating it against a later tree would destroy the only thing it is for.
# Reproduce it with:
#   git worktree add <dir> 2225c24d899729eadb12ae60e54682cd98068fa5
#   cd <dir> && python3 tools/validate_ledger.py --no-baseline
#
# 2307 error lines.
